c:T63a7,<div class="post-content">
<p>IBPS has thrown open the 16th edition of its clerical recruitment drive - CRP CSA-XVI - with 11,403 indicative vacancies for the Customer Service Associate post (the role most people still call "Clerk") across 11 participating public sector banks. Online registration runs from 1 August to 21 August 2026, covering hiring for the 2027-28 vacancy cycle.</p>
<p>This is a state/UT-wise recruitment, meaning you can apply against vacancies in only one state or union territory, so it's worth checking where the opportunities actually sit before you register.</p>

<h2>Application Fee</h2>
<ul>
<li>General / OBC / EWS : 850/- (Inclusive of GST)</li>
<li>SC / ST / PwBD / ESM : 175/- (Inclusive of GST)</li>
<li>Fee Is Non-Refundable Once Paid</li>
<li>Pay Through Debit Card, Credit Card, Net Banking or UPI Only - Bank Transaction Charges, if Any, Are Borne by the Candidate</li>
</ul>
<h2>Age Limit as on 01/08/2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Minimum Age : 20 Years</li>
<li>Maximum Age : 28 Years</li>
<li>Age Relaxation : SC/ST - 5 Years, OBC-NCL - 3 Years, PwBD - 10 Years, Ex-Servicemen - 5 Years (Cumulative With Only One Other Applicable Category)</li>
</ul>

<p>Note: Vacancies for Union Bank of India were not reported at the time this notification was released, so the final all-India total may change once those figures come in.</p>
<h2>Selection Process</h2>
<p>There's no interview at any stage of this recruitment - selection runs purely on your Prelims and Mains scores, followed by document verification. One thing that trips up a lot of first-time applicants: your Prelims marks don't carry forward into the final merit list. Prelims only decides who gets shortlisted for Mains; the Mains score alone determines your final rank.</p>
<ul>
<li>Preliminary Examination - 100 Questions / 100 Marks / 60 Minutes, Split Into English (30Q), Numerical Ability (35Q) and Reasoning Ability (35Q), Each With a 20-Minute Sectional Time Limit</li>
<li>Main Examination - 160 Questions / 200 Marks / 125 Minutes, Covering General/Financial Awareness (40Q/50M), General English (40Q/40M), Reasoning Ability (40Q/60M) and Quantitative Aptitude (40Q/50M)</li>
<li>Document Verification for Candidates Provisionally Allotted a Bank</li>
</ul>
<h2>Pay Scale</h2>
<p>Basic pay for the Customer Service Associate post starts at Rs. 24,050 and progresses to Rs. 64,480 over the course of increments. With allowances added in, the starting gross salary typically works out to around Rs. 40,000-42,000 a month, though this varies slightly by posting location.</p>
<h2>How to Fill IBPS Clerk CRP CSA-XVI Online Form 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Visit ibps.in, Open the CRP CSA-XVI Section and Click "New Registration" to Generate Your Provisional Registration Number and Password.</li>
<li>Upload Your Photograph, Signature, Left Thumb Impression, Handwritten Declaration and 10th/SSLC Certificate as per the Format Specified in Annexure IV of the Notification.</li>
<li>Fill the Form Section by Section Using "Save and Next," Since Saving Partway Through Lets You Come Back Without Losing Progress.</li>
<li>Pay the Fee Within the Same 01-21 August Window - a Saved but Unpaid Form Is Treated as an Invalid Application.</li>
<li>Double-Check That Your Payment Actually Went Through Before Closing the Tab - This Is a Common Point Where Applications Get Missed.</li>
<li>Print the Final Application Form and the Payment Receipt for Your Records.</li>
</ul>

<h2>IBPS Clerk CRP CSA-XVI Preparation Tips 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Since Prelims Marks Don't Count Toward the Final Merit, Treat It as a Speed Test - Focus on Clearing Sectional Cutoffs Comfortably Rather Than Maximizing Your Score There.</li>
<li>Mains Is Where the Real Battle Happens - Quantitative Aptitude and Reasoning Together Carry 110 of the 200 Marks, So Prioritize Them in Your Daily Practice.</li>
<li>For General/Financial Awareness, Track Recent RBI Policy Changes, Repo Rate Movements and Major Banking Sector News From the Last 4-6 Months.</li>
<li>Work on Speed in English - With Only 35 Minutes for 40 Questions in Mains, Reading Comprehension Passages Need to Be Tackled Efficiently, Not Read Word by Word.</li>
<li>Practice Local Language Basics if It Isn't Your Mother Tongue, Since Proficiency in the State/UT's Local Language Is a Stated Eligibility Requirement.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2>
<section class="tre-faq-list"><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the last date to apply for IBPS Clerk CRP CSA-XVI 2026?</summary>
<p>The last date to apply online, along with the fee payment deadline, is 21 August 2026.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>How many total vacancies are there in IBPS Clerk 2026?</summary>
<p>A total of 11,403 indicative vacancies have been announced across participating states and union territories, though this figure may change once Union Bank of India reports its numbers.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Is there an interview in the IBPS Clerk selection process?</summary>
<p>No, there is no interview. Selection is based entirely on the Preliminary and Main Examination scores, followed by document verification.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Do Prelims marks count toward the final selection?</summary>
<p>No, Prelims is only a qualifying stage to shortlist candidates for Mains. The final merit list is prepared solely on the basis of Mains marks.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the application fee for IBPS Clerk 2026?</summary>
<p>General, OBC and EWS candidates need to pay Rs. 850/-, while SC, ST, PwBD and Ex-Servicemen candidates pay Rs. 175/-, both figures inclusive of GST.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Can a candidate apply for vacancies in more than one state?</summary>
<p>No, recruitment under CRP CSA-XVI is conducted state/UT-wise, and a candidate can apply for vacancies in only one state or union territory.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the age limit for IBPS Clerk CRP CSA-XVI 2026?</summary>
<p>Candidates must be between 20 and 28 years of age as on 01 August 2026, with standard age relaxation applicable for reserved categories.</p>
</details></section>

<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q1.</span> The repo rate, a key tool used by RBI to control inflation, refers to the rate at which:</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="ibps_q1_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">RBI lends short-term funds to commercial banks against securities</li>
<li id="ibps_q1_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Commercial banks lend to each other overnight</li>
<li id="ibps_q1_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">RBI borrows money from commercial banks</li>
<li id="ibps_q1_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Banks lend to their retail customers</li>
</ul>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">

<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="ibps_q2_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">The bank's ability to absorb losses relative to its risk-weighted assets</li>
<li id="ibps_q2_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">The bank's total deposit base</li>
<li id="ibps_q2_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">The interest rate charged on loans</li>
<li id="ibps_q2_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">The number of branches a bank operates</li>
</ul>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">

<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="ibps_q3_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">It cannot be honoured by the bank before the date mentioned on it</li>
<li id="ibps_q3_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">It becomes invalid immediately</li>
<li id="ibps_q3_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">It can be cashed at any bank regardless of date</li>

<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q18.</span> Choose the most appropriate synonym for 'Prudent' as it would be used to describe sound financial decision-making:</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">

</div>d:T6334,<div class="post-content">
<p>Bihar Public Service Commission (BPSC) has opened OTR registration for <strong>School Teacher TRE 4.0 Recruitment 2026</strong>, covering 32388 posts across Primary, Middle, TGT and PGT categories. Candidates interested in Class 1 to 5, Class 6 to 8, Class 9 to 10, Class 11 to 12 and Special School Teacher posts can apply as per the schedule. Read the notification for eligibility, post information, selection procedure, age limit and all other information before applying it.</p>
<p>Bihar Public Service Commission has invited online applications for BPSC School Teacher TRE 4.0 Recruitment 2026. Interested candidates should complete OTR (One Time Registration) first, then apply online once the application window opens. Read the notification carefully before applying and check all eligibility conditions, post information, selection procedure, age limit and other details mentioned below.</p>

<ul>

<li>SC / ST / PH : 100/-</li>
<li>Fee Payment Accepted Through Online or Offline Mode Only</li>
</ul>
<h2>Age Limit</h2>
<ul>
<li>Minimum Age (Primary Teacher) : 18 Years</li>
<li>Minimum Age (TGT / PGT Teacher) : 21 Years</li>
<li>Maximum Age : 37 Years for Male Candidates, 40 Years for Female Candidates</li>
<li>Category-Wise Age Relaxation Is Detailed in the Official Notification - Check It Before Assuming Your Exact Cutoff</li>
</ul>

<h2>Important Points Before Filling the BPSC TRE 4.0 Form</h2>
<ul>
<li>Complete Your BPSC OTR Registration Right Away Rather Than Waiting for the Application Window to Open - It Saves Time Once Forms Actually Go Live.</li>
<li>Candidates Currently Appearing in Bihar STET / CTET (Results Awaited) Are Not Eligible to Fill This Form.</li>
<li>In Place of the Bihar STET Result Card Number, You'll Need to Enter Your BSEB Unique ID During the Application.</li>
<li>CTET Candidates Should Use the Serial Number Printed on Their Certificate or Marksheet.</li>
<li>There's No Need to Physically Send Any Application Copy to BPSC - Everything Is Handled Online.</li>
<li>Read the Complete TRE 4.0 Notification for Anything Not Covered Here.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Selection Process</h2>
<p>Selection under TRE 4.0 works on a single-stage, merit-based model rather than multiple rounds of interviews. Since STET/CTET has already screened candidates for subject and teaching eligibility, BPSC's own test focuses on ranking rather than re-testing basic qualification.</p>
<ul>
<li>Computer Based Test (CBT) - Objective Type Questions Covering General Studies, Language and Subject-Related Areas Depending on the Post</li>
<li>Document Verification for Candidates Who Clear the CBT Cutoff</li>
<li>Final Merit List Prepared Based on CBT Score, District and Category-Wise Preferences</li>
</ul>
<h2>How to Fill BPSC School Teacher TRE 4.0 Online Form 2026</h2>
<p>If you haven't registered on the BPSC OTR portal yet, that's the first thing to sort out - it's a one-time process and once it's done, filling the actual TRE 4.0 application becomes much quicker. BPSC has issued this notification for Primary Teacher, Trained Graduate Teacher (TGT) and Post Graduate Teacher (PGT) posts, and candidates will be able to apply online once the exact schedule is announced.</p>
<ul>
<li>Go Through the Notification Carefully Before You Begin Filling the Form.</li>
<li>Keep Your Documents Ready in Advance - Handwriting Sample, Educational Certificates, ID Proof, Address Proof and Basic Details.</li>
<li>Scan Copies of Your Photo, Signature, ID Proof and Thumb Impression Should Be Ready in the Prescribed Format.</li>
<li>Go Through Every Column in the Preview Section Carefully Before Final Submission.</li>
<li>Application Fee Payment Is Mandatory - Without It, Your Form Stays Incomplete.</li>
<li>Save or Print the Confirmation Page Once You've Submitted the Form.</li>
</ul>
<p>Interested Candidates Should Read the Full Notification Before Applying Online.</p>

<h2>Bihar School Teacher TRE 4.0 Preparation Tips 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Since STET / CTET Qualification Is Already a Prerequisite, Focus Your Prep on the CBT Stage - Child Development &amp; Pedagogy, General Studies and Subject Knowledge Carry Most of the Weight.</li>
<li>For Primary and Middle School Posts, Revisit NCERT Books From Class 6 to 10 for Subject Content, Since Questions Often Stay Close to the School Syllabus.</li>
<li>TGT and PGT Aspirants Should Go Deeper Into Their Specific Subject at Graduation / Post-Graduation Level, Along With Bihar-Specific General Knowledge.</li>
<li>Practice Previous Year TRE Papers (Phase 1, 2 and 3) to Get a Sense of the Question Pattern BPSC Tends to Repeat.</li>
<li>Keep Your OTR Details, Category Certificates and Educational Documents Updated and Error-Free Well Before the Form Opens, Since Corrections Later Can Get Complicated.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2>
<section class="tre-faq-list"><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Q1. What is BPSC TRE 4.0?</summary>
<p>BPSC TRE 4.0 is the fourth phase of the Bihar Teacher Recruitment Examination, conducted by the Bihar Public Service Commission to fill Primary, Middle, TGT and PGT teacher posts across the state.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Q2. How many total vacancies are there in BPSC TRE 4.0?</summary>
<p>A total of 32388 posts have been announced across Primary School Teacher, Middle School Teacher, TGT Teacher and PGT Teacher categories.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Q3. When will the BPSC TRE 4.0 application form start?</summary>
<p>The application process is expected to begin in July or August 2026, though the exact date will be confirmed in the official schedule.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Q4. Is STET or CTET compulsory for BPSC TRE 4.0?</summary>
<p>Yes, STET or CTET/BTET qualification (Paper I or Paper II depending on the post) is required. However, candidates currently appearing in STET or CTET with results awaited cannot fill this form.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Q5. What documents are needed instead of the STET Result Card Number?</summary>
<p>Candidates need to enter their BSEB Unique ID in place of the Bihar STET Result Card Number while filling the application.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Q6. What is the application fee for BPSC TRE 4.0?</summary>
<p>The fee is Rs. 100/- for all categories, including General, OBC, SC, ST and PH candidates, payable through online or offline mode.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Q7. Do candidates need to send a physical copy of the application to BPSC?</summary>
<p>No, the entire process is online and there is no requirement to send any application copy by post to BPSC.</p>
</details></section>

</div>e:T4f96,<div class="post-content">
<p>Railway Recruitment Board (RRB) is conducting recruitment for <strong>Technician Grade-I Signal and Technician Grade-III</strong> posts under CEN No. 02/2026, with 6557 vacancies on offer. Applications open on 30 June 2026 and close on 29 July 2026.</p>

<h2>Application Fee</h2>
<ul>
<li>General / OBC / EWS : 500/-</li>
<li>SC / ST : 250/-</li>
<li>PH (Divyang) : 250/-</li>
<li>Partial Refund Applies Once a Candidate Has Appeared for the Stage I Exam - UR/OBC/EWS Get Rs. 400/- Back, While SC/ST/PH/Female Candidates Get Rs. 250/- Back</li>
<li>Fee Can Be Paid via E Challan (Cash) or Online Through Debit Card, Credit Card or Net Banking</li>
</ul>
<h2>Age Limit as on 01/07/2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Minimum Age : 18 Years</li>
<li>Technician Grade-III : Up to 30 Years</li>
<li>Technician Grade-I Signal : Up to 33 Years</li>
<li>Standard Age Relaxation Applies as per RRB Technician CEN 02/2026 Rules</li>
</ul>

<h2>RRB Technician Preparation Tips 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Build a Strong Base in General Science and General Awareness First..</li>
<li>Practice Mathematics and Reasoning Every Day With a Timer Running.</li>
<li>Grade-I Signal Aspirants Should Revisit Their Core Subjects (Physics, Electronics, Instrumentation) Alongside General Topics.</li>
<li>Grade-III Candidates Should Brush Up on Their ITI Trade Fundamentals, Since Trade-Specific Questions Do Appear.</li>
<li>Practice&nbsp; Few Previous Year RRB Technician or ALP Papers.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2>
<section class="tre-faq-list"><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the last date to apply for RRB Technician CEN 02/2026?</summary>
<p>The last date to apply online is 29 July 2026, and the fee payment deadline is also 29 July 2026.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>How many total vacancies are there in RRB Technician CEN 02/2026?</summary>
<p>A total of 6557 posts have been announced, with 323 for Technician Grade-I Signal and 6234 for Technician Grade-III (Open Line).</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the application fee for General category candidates?</summary>
<p>General, OBC and EWS candidates need to pay Rs. 500/-, while SC, ST and PH (Divyang) candidates pay Rs. 250/-.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Is the application fee refundable?</summary>
<p>Yes, a partial refund applies once a candidate has appeared for the Stage I exam - UR/OBC/EWS get Rs. 400/- back, while SC/ST/PH/Female candidates get Rs. 250/- back.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the age limit for Technician Grade-III and Grade-I Signal posts?</summary>
<p>The minimum age is 18 years for both posts. Grade-III has a maximum age of 30 years, while Grade-I Signal goes up to 33 years, as on 01 July 2026.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the minimum qualification for Technician Grade-III (Open Line)?</summary>
<p>Candidates need a Class 10th pass certificate along with an ITI Certificate (NCVT/SCVT) in a related trade or branch.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>When will the exam city and admit card be available?</summary>
<p>The exam city intimation will be available 10 days before the exam, and the admit card will be released 4 days before the exam.</p>
</details></section>

<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q20.</span> Which safety equipment is mandatory while carrying out welding or fitting work, a common technical/safety question in RRB trade papers?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Regular sunglasses</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Safety goggles and protective gloves</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">A raincoat</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Ordinary cotton gloves without insulation</li>
</ul>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q21.</span> What is the relationship between voltage, current, and resistance according to Ohm's Law, a core concept for Signal Technician candidates?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Voltage = Current x Resistance squared</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Voltage = Current x Resistance</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Voltage = Current divided by Resistance</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Voltage has no relation to Resistance</li>
</ul>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q22.</span> Which material property is most important when selecting cable insulation in railway signalling systems?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">High electrical conductivity</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-right">High resistance to electrical current (good insulating property)</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Magnetic property</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Low melting point</li>
</ul>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q23.</span> If a Grade-III candidate's category is SC/ST, what application fee applies to them under this CEN?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Rs. 500/-</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Rs. 250/-</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">No fee at all</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Rs. 100/-</li>
</ul>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q24.</span> In a Fitter/Machinist trade context, which tool is used to check whether a surface is perfectly flat?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Hacksaw</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Surface plate and spirit level</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Screwdriver</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Wrench</li>
</ul>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q25.</span> A candidate has both a B.Sc in Electronics and a Diploma in Instrumentation. Which post are they most likely eligible for under this CEN?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Technician Grade-III only, since higher qualifications are not counted</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Technician Grade-I Signal, since this qualification matches its eligibility criteria</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Neither post, as this combination isn't recognized</li>
<li class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Only clerical posts, not technical ones</li>
</ul>
</article>

<h2>AAI Manager, Junior Executive Preparation Tips 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Focus on Core Subject Knowledge Related to Your Applied Post (Finance, Engineering, Law, Operations Etc.).</li>
<li>Cover General Awareness Topics Related to Aviation Sector, Airports Authority of India and Current Affairs of the Last 6 Months.</li>
<li>Revise Basic Reasoning and Quantitative Aptitude Daily, Since These Sections Are Common Across Most CBT-Based Exams.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2>
<section class="tre-faq-list"><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the last date to apply for AAI Manager, Junior Executive Recruitment 2026?</summary>
<p>The last date to apply online is 07 September 2026, and the fee payment deadline is also 07 September 2026.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>How many total vacancies are there in AAI Recruitment 2026?</summary>
<p>A total of 389 posts have been announced across various Junior Executive and Manager categories, including Finance, Law, Operations, Engineering, IT, Architecture and more.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the application fee for AAI Manager, Junior Executive 2026?</summary>
<p>General, OBC and EWS candidates need to pay Rs. 1000/-, while SC, ST, PH and female candidates are exempted from the fee.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the age limit for Junior Executive and Manager posts?</summary>
<p>The maximum age is 27 years for Junior Executive posts and 32 years for Manager posts, as on 07 September 2026, with age relaxation applicable as per rules.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Is work experience required for Manager posts in AAI?</summary>
<p>Yes, most Manager posts require 3 years of relevant experience along with the required educational qualification, while Junior Executive posts generally do not require prior experience.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the selection process for AAI Manager, Junior Executive 2026?</summary>
<p>Selection involves a Computer Based Test (CBT), followed by Document Verification and an Interview or Skill Test wherever applicable for the specific post.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Which post has the highest number of vacancies in this AAI recruitment?</summary>
<p>Manager (Engineering - Civil) has the highest number of vacancies with 145 posts, followed by Junior Executive (Operations) with 79 posts.</p>

<li>Apply Early Within the Window to Avoid Last-Minute Server Issues on the Application Portal.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2>
<section class="tre-faq-list"><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the last date to apply for RRC Prayagraj Apprentice Recruitment 2026?</summary>
<p>The last date to apply online is 07 August 2026.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>How many total vacancies are there in this apprentice recruitment?</summary>
<p>A total of 1853 posts have been announced for Various Trade Apprentice positions.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Is there an application fee for SC/ST and female candidates?</summary>
<p>No, SC/ST candidates and all category female candidates are exempted from the application fee.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the minimum qualification required for RRC Prayagraj Apprentice posts?</summary>
<p>Candidates need a Class 10 pass certificate with minimum 50% marks along with an ITI/NCVT certificate in a related trade.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Is there a written exam for this apprentice recruitment?</summary>
<p>No, selection is based on a merit list prepared from Class 10 and ITI marks, followed by document verification.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the age limit for RRC Prayagraj Apprentice Recruitment 2026?</summary>
<p>Candidates must be between 15 and 24 years of age as on 07 July 2026, with age relaxation as per rules.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Where can candidates check the trade-wise and division-wise vacancy breakup?</summary>
<p>The complete trade-wise and division-wise vacancy details are provided in the official notification released by RRC Prayagraj.</p>
</details></section>

</div>13:Td85a,<div class="post-content">
<p>Railway Recruitment Board (RRB) has released the notification for <strong>Junior Engineer (JE), Depot Material Superintendent and Chemical &amp; Metallurgical Assistant Recruitment 2026</strong> under CEN No. 04/2026. Candidates interested in this RRB JE Recruitment can apply online from 14 August 2026 to 13 September 2026.&nbsp;</p>

<h2>Application Fee</h2>
<ul>
<li>General / OBC / EWS : 500/-</li>
<li>SC / ST / PH : 250/-</li>
<li>All Category Female : 250/-</li>
<li>Refund Is Applicable After Candidates Appear for the Stage I Exam</li>
<li>General / OBC / EWS Fee Refund : Rs. 400/-</li>
<li>SC / ST / PH / Female Refund : Rs. 250/-</li>
<li>Pay the Exam Fee Through E Challan, Debit Card, Credit Card or Net Banking</li>
</ul>

<ul>

<li>Maximum Age : 33 Years</li>
<li>Age Relaxation Extra as per RRB JE Junior Engineer Recruitment Rules</li>
</ul>

<h2>RRB JE Junior Engineer Preparation Tips 2026</h2>
<ul>
<li>Cover General Awareness like Current Affairs, Static GK and Railway Related Topics.</li>
<li>Practice Mathematics Daily on the Topics of Percentage, Ratio, Time &amp; Work, Algebra and Geometry.</li>
<li>Revise General Intelligence &amp; Reasoning Through Puzzles, Series, Coding-Decoding and Analogy on a Regular Basis.</li>
<li>For Stage II, Focus on Your Core Engineering Discipline Subjects.</li>
<li>Check and try to solve previous Year RRB JE Papers to Understand the Exact Question Pattern and Difficulty Level.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2>
<section class="tre-faq-list"><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the last date to apply for RRB JE CEN 04/2026?</summary>
<p>The last date to apply online is 13 September 2026, and the fee payment deadline is also 13 September 2026.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>How many total vacancies are there in RRB JE CEN 04/2026?</summary>
<p>A total of 4098 posts have been announced across Junior Engineer, Depot Material Superintendent and Chemical &amp; Metallurgical Assistant categories.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the application fee for General category candidates?</summary>
<p>General, OBC and EWS candidates need to pay Rs. 500/-, while SC, ST, PH and all category female candidates pay Rs. 250/-.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Is the application fee refundable in RRB JE CEN 04/2026?</summary>
<p>Yes, a refund applies once a candidate has appeared for the Stage I exam - General/OBC/EWS get Rs. 400/- back, while SC/ST/PH/Female candidates get Rs. 250/- back.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the age limit for RRB JE CEN 04/2026?</summary>
<p>Candidates must be between 18 and 33 years of age as on 01 January 2027, with standard age relaxation applicable as per RRB rules.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the minimum qualification required for RRB JE posts?</summary>
<p>Candidates generally need a Diploma or Degree in Engineering in the relevant discipline, though 10+2 with Science may also apply for certain posts as per the specific post requirement.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>Where can candidates check the category-wise vacancy breakup for RRB JE?</summary>
<p>The category-wise (UR/OBC/EWS/SC/ST) vacancy numbers are yet to be notified and will be updated once released in the official notification.</p>
</details></section>

</details><details class="tre-faq-item">

</details><details class="tre-faq-item">
<summary>What is the age limit for JSSC JILCCE Inter Level 2026?</summary>
<p>Candidates must be between 18 and 35 years of age, with age relaxation applicable as per JSSC rules.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">

<p>Candidates need to have passed 10+2 (Intermediate) from any recognized board in India.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">

<p>The correction window is open from 21 to 23 August 2026 for candidates who need to make changes to their submitted application.</p>
</details><details class="tre-faq-item">

<p>The Directorate of Health Services has the highest number of vacancies through the Health Supervisor post, with 137 openings.</p>

<li>Keep All Documents and Photo (as per Instructions).</li>
</ul>

<h2>Rajasthan High Court Stenographer Preparation Tips 2026</h2>
<h3>1. Shorthand (Most Important)</h3>
<ul>
<li>Practice Daily: Start Slow from 40-50 WPM and Gradually Increase. Aim to Increase Speed by 5 WPM Every Week Until You Reach 80+.WPM&nbsp;</li>
<li>Transcription Matters Too: Writing Fast Is Not Enough, You Must Be Able to Transcribe It Back Into Accurate English Without Errors or using Backspace.</li>
<li>Dedicate a Minimum of 1-2 Hours Daily necause typing Built on Muscle Memory so taking a Break Causes Speed to Drop.</li>
</ul>
<h3>2. English (Highest Weightage in the Written Exam)</h3>
<ul>
<li>Grammar: Practice 10-15 Questions Daily on Tenses, Articles, Prepositions and Verb.</li>
<li>Vocabulary: Learn 10 New Words Daily With Their Synonyms and Antonyms.</li>
<li>Comprehension: Read 2-3 Passages a Week and Solve Questions on Them, This Also Improves Reading speed.</li>
</ul>
<h3>3. Reasoning (A Speed Test)</h3>
<ul>
<li>Series, Coding-Decoding, Analogy, Blood Relations (These Are All Pattern Based, So the More You Practice the Faster You Will Solve Them).</li>
<li>Practice With a Timer because Speed Matters in the Real Exam.</li>
</ul>
<h3>4. General Awareness</h3>
<ul>
<li>Current Affairs: Cover the Last 6 Months Current Affairs by Read Newspaper or a Current Affairs in daily basis for at least 30 to 60 Minutes.</li>
<li>Static GK: Revise History, Polity and Geography a Little Every Day.</li>
</ul>

<h2>WBPSC ICDS Supervisor Preparation Tips 2026</h2>
<h3>1. Prelims - General Knowledge</h3>
<ul>
<li>Focus on West Bengal Specific GK like State History, Geography, Culture and Current Affairs.</li>
<li>Cover National Current Affairs of the Last 6 Months.</li>
<li>Read Newspaper or Current Affairs for 15 to 20 Minutes Daily and try to Make Short Notes for Quick Revision.</li>
</ul>
<h3>2. Prelims - Arithmetic</h3>
<ul>
<li>Practice Basic Topics Daily: Percentage, Ratio, Average, Profit &amp; Loss, Simple/Compound Interest and Time &amp; Work.</li>
<li>Since the Paper Is Objective With a 60-Minute Time Limit for 100 Questions, Speed and Accuracy Both Matter - Practice With a Timer.</li>
<li>Solve 20-25 Questions Daily, Covering Mixed Topics From Previous Year Papers.</li>
</ul>
<h3>3. Prelims - Reasoning</h3>
<ul>
<li>Practice Series, Coding-Decoding, Analogy, Blood Relations, Direction Sense and Puzzles for Regular basis to Improves Speed.</li>
</ul>
<h3>4. Mains - Descriptive Paper</h3>
<ul>
<li>The Mains Paper Is 400 Marks With 90 Minutes Per Paper so Practice Writing Answers Within the Time Limit.</li>
<li>Read About ICDS Scheme, Women &amp; Child Development Policies and Social Welfare Schemes of West Bengal because These Are Directly Related to the Post.</li>
<li>Practice Bengali Language Sections Regularly because Proficiency in Bengali Is Generally Required for the Supervisor Post.</li>
</ul>
<h3>5. Viva-Voce (Personality Test)</h3>
<ul>
<li>Ready to Answer Questions on the ICDS Scheme, Your Educational Background and Why You Want This Role.</li>
<li>Stay Updated on Current Affairs Related to Women &amp; Child Development.</li>
<li>Practice Mock Interviews to Build Confidence and Work on Clear Communication.</li>
<li>Attempt at Least One Full Mock Test Weekly toTrack Progress.</li>
<li>Keep Revising Previous Year Question Papers because Repeated Patterns Are Common in State-Level Exams.</li>
</ul>

<section class="tre-quiz-wrap">
<h3 class="tre-quiz-header">WBPSC ICDS Supervisor - Job Specific Practice Questions (ICDS Scheme, Anganwadi Structure &amp; Social Welfare)</h3>
<p class="tre-quiz-sub">25 Objective Questions with Explanation</p>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q1.</span> An ICDS Supervisor is generally responsible for overseeing how many Anganwadi Centres on average in a project area?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q1_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">5 to 8</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q1_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Around 20 to 25</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q1_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">50 to 60</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q1_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">100 and above</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> A Supervisor (also called Mukhya Sevika) typically monitors around 20-25 Anganwadi Centres under one ICDS project/circle, conducting regular visits, checking registers and guiding the Anganwadi Workers.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q2.</span> Which register is NOT usually maintained at an Anganwadi Centre?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q2_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Growth Monitoring Register</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q2_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Attendance Register</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q2_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Beneficiary Register (Pregnant/Lactating Women)</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q2_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Land Revenue Register</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> Anganwadi records mainly track children, mothers, health check-ups, nutrition and attendance - not land revenue, which falls under a different department entirely.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q3.</span> The Take Home Ration (THR) under ICDS is generally provided to which beneficiaries?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q3_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Only school-going children above 10 years</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q3_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Children 6 months to 3 years, and pregnant/lactating mothers</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q3_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Only senior citizens</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q3_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Only Anganwadi staff</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> THR is meant for children who cannot yet eat cooked meals at the centre (6 months to 3 years) and for pregnant and lactating mothers, so they can consume nutrition at home.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q4.</span> Which of these is a core service under the ICDS package (also called the "Six Services")?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q4_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Vocational Skill Training for Adults</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q4_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Non-Formal Pre-School Education</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q4_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Loan Distribution</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q4_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Pension Disbursement</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> The ICDS "six services" are supplementary nutrition, immunization, health check-up, referral services, non-formal pre-school education, and nutrition &amp; health education.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q5.</span> Who is the immediate reporting authority above an ICDS Supervisor at the block level in West Bengal?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q5_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Child Development Project Officer (CDPO)</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q5_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">District Magistrate</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q5_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Block Development Officer only</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q5_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">State Secretary</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> The CDPO heads the ICDS project at the block level and Supervisors report to the CDPO, who further reports up the district and state administrative chain.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q6.</span> Which tool is commonly used by Anganwadi Workers to assess a child's nutritional status during growth monitoring?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q6_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Weighing scale and height chart against growth cards</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q6_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Blood pressure monitor</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q6_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">ECG machine</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q6_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">X-ray machine</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> Growth monitoring under ICDS relies on regular weight and height recording, plotted against standard growth cards to identify malnourished or at-risk children early.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q7.</span> Which category of children is specifically prioritized for referral to a Nutrition Rehabilitation Centre (NRC)?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q7_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Children with normal growth</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q7_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Severely Acute Malnourished (SAM) children</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q7_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Children above 12 years</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q7_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Only children with eye problems</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> SAM children need intensive medical and nutritional care, so ICDS field staff refer them to NRCs where they receive supervised treatment until their condition stabilizes.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q8.</span> The Scheme for Adolescent Girls under the Women &amp; Child Development set-up mainly targets which age group?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q8_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">0 to 3 years</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q8_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">3 to 6 years</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q8_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">11 to 14 years (out-of-school girls in most versions)</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q8_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Above 25 years</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> This scheme focuses on nutrition, health awareness, and life-skill support for adolescent girls, particularly those who have dropped out of formal schooling.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q9.</span> Which of the following comes under the Social Welfare Department's mandate along with ICDS in West Bengal?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q9_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Welfare schemes for differently-abled persons and destitute women</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q9_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Highway construction</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q9_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Power distribution</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q9_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Railway ticketing</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> Along with child and maternal welfare, the department also runs programs for the differently-abled, orphaned children, and destitute or distressed women.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q10.</span> What does the term "VHSND" (commonly linked with Anganwadi activities) stand for?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q10_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Village Health, Sanitation and Nutrition Day</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q10_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Vocational Health Skill Naming Drive</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q10_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Village Housing Scheme for Nutrition Development</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q10_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Village Health Survey and Numbering Data</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> VHSND is a monthly outreach event held at the Anganwadi level, bringing together ASHA, ANM and Anganwadi Workers for immunization, health check-ups and nutrition counselling.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q11.</span> As per general ICDS norms, what is the minimum number of days an Anganwadi Centre should ideally remain functional in a month?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q11_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">10 days</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q11_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">15 days</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q11_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">25 days</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q11_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">30 days, no holidays</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> Anganwadi Centres are generally expected to function around 25 days a month, excluding weekly offs and government holidays, to keep nutrition and pre-school activities consistent.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q12.</span> Which of these duties would typically fall to a Supervisor rather than an Anganwadi Worker?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q12_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Serving cooked meals daily</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q12_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Conducting on-the-spot inspection and verifying stock registers</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q12_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Cleaning the centre premises</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q12_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Taking daily attendance of children</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> Field-level duties like cooking or attendance are handled by the Worker/Helper, while inspection, verification, and guidance across multiple centres fall under the Supervisor's role.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q13.</span> The Beti Bachao Beti Padhao scheme is administered in coordination with which ministries?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q13_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Women &amp; Child Development, Health, and Education</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q13_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Defence and Home Affairs</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q13_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Railways and Civil Aviation</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q13_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Commerce and Industry</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> Since the scheme addresses declining child sex ratio and girls' education together, it is run jointly through the WCD, Health and Education ministries.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q14.</span> Under the Mid-Day Meal / nutrition linkage, which age group is generally NOT covered by the Anganwadi feeding programme but by school-based schemes instead?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q14_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">6 months to 3 years</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q14_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">3 to 6 years</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q14_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Primary school-going children (6+ years)</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q14_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Pregnant women</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> Once a child moves into formal primary schooling, nutrition support generally shifts from the Anganwadi's supplementary feeding to the school Mid-Day Meal Scheme.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q15.</span> What is the primary purpose of the Nutrition and Health Education (NHE) component of ICDS?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q15_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Building awareness among mothers on childcare, hygiene and diet practices</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q15_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Providing free housing loans</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q15_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Distributing agricultural equipment</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q15_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Conducting driving license tests</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> NHE sessions equip mothers and caregivers with practical knowledge on feeding practices, hygiene, and preventive healthcare for children and themselves.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q16.</span> Which of the following is generally a disqualifying factor while filling the WBPSC ICDS application, unless specifically permitted?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q16_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Providing false information in the OTR profile</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q16_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Applying from a cyber caf&eacute;</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q16_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Uploading photo in JPEG format</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q16_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Applying a day before the deadline</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> Submitting incorrect or false details in the One Time Registration profile can lead to rejection of candidature at any later stage of the recruitment process.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q17.</span> In the ICDS structure, what is the role of an "Anganwadi Helper" distinct from a "Worker"?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q17_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Assisting with meal preparation, cleanliness and bringing children to the centre</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q17_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Handling the block-level budget</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q17_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Approving maternity benefit applications</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q17_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Conducting the Supervisor's exam</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> The Helper supports the Worker with day-to-day physical tasks at the centre, while planning, teaching and record-keeping remain the Worker's responsibility.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q18.</span> A batch of registers at an Anganwadi Centre shows 60 enrolled children, of whom 45 attended on a given day. What is the attendance percentage that day?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q18_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">65%</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q18_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">70%</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q18_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">75%</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q18_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">80%</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> 45 out of 60 = 45/60 x 100 = 75%. Such register-based percentage calculation questions are common in the ICDS Prelims arithmetic section.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q19.</span> If a Supervisor covers 24 Anganwadi Centres in 6 working days, visiting an equal number each day, how many centres does she cover per day?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q19_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">2</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q19_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">3</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q19_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">4</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q19_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">6</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> 24 centres divided across 6 days = 4 centres per day, a straightforward division-based question framed around real field duties.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q20.</span> A nutrition budget of ₹1,20,000 is to be distributed equally among 8 Anganwadi Centres in a circle. How much does each centre receive?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q20_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">₹12,000</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q20_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">₹15,000</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q20_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">₹16,000</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q20_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">₹18,000</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> ₹1,20,000 &divide; 8 = ₹15,000 per centre. Budget-distribution style word problems are frequently framed around welfare-department scenarios in this exam.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q21.</span> Out of 200 children screened during a growth monitoring drive, 15% were found moderately underweight. How many children does that represent?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q21_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">25</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q21_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">30</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q21_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">35</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q21_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">40</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> 15% of 200 = 30 children. Percentage-based questions tied to health screening data are a common format in the ICDS/Social Welfare paper.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q22.</span> If Anganwadi Worker, Helper, and Supervisor posts are advertised in the ratio 6:3:1, and there are 60 Worker posts, how many Supervisor posts are there?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q22_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">8</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q22_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">10</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q22_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">12</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q22_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">15</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> If 6 parts = 60, then 1 part = 10, so Supervisor posts (1 part) = 10. Ratio questions like this reflect how vacancy break-ups are often presented in real notifications.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q23.</span> A Supervisor starts her centre visits from the CDPO office, travels east to Centre A, then turns south to reach Centre B. In which direction must she travel to return directly to the CDPO office?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q23_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">South-East</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q23_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">North-West</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q23_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">South-West</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q23_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">North-East</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> Moving east then south takes her away from the start point diagonally; the direct return path would be the opposite diagonal, i.e. north-west.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q24.</span> In a coded register, "AWC" (Anganwadi Centre) is written as "CYE" by shifting each letter forward by 2. Using the same logic, how would "CDPO" be coded?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q24_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">DERP</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q24_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">EFRQ</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q24_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">EFQR</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q24_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">DFRQ</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> Shifting each letter of C-D-P-O forward by 2 gives E-F-R-Q, following the same +2 pattern used to convert AWC into CYE.</p>
</article>
<article class="tre-q-card">
<p class="tre-q-text"><span class="tre-q-num">Q25.</span> Four documents are required for the ICDS application: Domicile Certificate, Educational Certificate, Caste Certificate, and Nutrition Chart. Which one does NOT belong with the rest in this list?</p>
<ul class="tre-quiz-options">
<li id="wbpsc_q25_o1" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Domicile Certificate</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q25_o2" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Educational Certificate</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q25_o3" class="tre-opt-item opt-wrong">Caste Certificate</li>
<li id="wbpsc_q25_o4" class="tre-opt-item opt-right">Nutrition Chart</li>
</ul>
<p class="tre-q-explain"><strong>Explanation:</strong> The first three are personal eligibility documents needed at the time of applying; a Nutrition Chart is a field-level ICDS record, not an application document.</p>
</article>
</section>

<p>Railway Recruitment Board (RRB) has released a fresh notification for the post of <strong>Section Controller</strong> under Centralised Employment Notification No. <strong>CEN 03/2026</strong>. Those candidates who have been enrolled with RRB Section Controller CEN 03/2026 exam can apply online from 15/07/2026 to 14/08/2026.</p>
